CatRetriever therefore provides a scalable route for connecting catalyst generative models with physically plausible and adsorption energy compatible bulk catalyst discovery.

</details>


### [160] [Active Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11720)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL）框架下，通过主动策略选择来优化有限在线交互预算分配的方法，在评估与微调之间取得平衡，实验证明其优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准O2O-RL流程离线训练多个候选策略后，仅选择单一策略进行在线微调，但微调性能对算法和超参数高度敏感，导致决策风险较高。现有方法缺乏在有限交互预算下主动选择策略的机制。

Method: 将问题形式化为在线交互在策略评估与微调之间的权衡，提出基于上置信界（UCB）的主动策略选择方法。该方法通过局部线性性能预测模型（拟合自在线评估观测数据）推导未微调策略的未来性能上界，从而动态选择最有潜力的策略进行微调。

Result: 在多样化实验设置下，所提方法一致优于现有O2O-RL基线，包括选择单一最佳策略和平均分配预算两种策略。

Conclusion: 主动选择并微调策略能更高效地利用有限在线交互预算，推动离线RL向实际高风险/高成本系统的部署迈进。

Abstract: Background: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables effective policies to be trained from large, previously collected datasets and subsequently improved through limited online interaction. This offline-to-online RL (O2O-RL) paradigm is particularly promising in nonstationary domains where interaction is costly or potentially hazardous. Standard O2O-RL pipelines train multiple candidate policies offline, evaluate them using off-policy or online evaluation, and then deploy and fine-tune the policy with the highest estimated value. However, as in offline pretraining, fine-tuning performance is highly sensitive to the choice of algorithm and hyperparameters, making it risky to commit to a single policy. Objectives: We study active policy selection for fine-tuning under a limited interaction budget in O2O-RL settings. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address this problem. Methods: We formulate the problem by identifying a fundamental trade-off between allocating online interactions to policy evaluation, which helps identify high-performing policies, and allocating them to fine-tuning, which improves policy performance. We then propose an approach that balances this trade-off by actively selecting policies for fine-tuning based on upper-confidence bounds on their future performance. These bounds are derived from locally linear performance forecasts fitted to observations obtained through online evaluation. Results: Across a diverse range of experiments, the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing O2O-RL baselines. Conclusions: Actively selecting and fine-tuning policies uses limited online interaction budgets more effectively than either committing to a single policy or dividing the budget equally among all policies. Our framework also advances offline RL toward practical deployment in real-world systems where online interaction is costly or risky.

</details>


### [161] [Time-Lag-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job-Shop Scheduling in PPVC Module Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11725)
*Ziheng Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对预制化模块建筑生产中的长后操作时滞问题（如混凝土养护、防水测试、油漆干燥），通过三项最小侵入式扩展改进双注意力深度强化学习求解器，使其与约束规划基准的差距缩小至约4%，优于所有调度规则和遗传算法元启发式，且能快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预制化模块建筑的生产车间作为柔性作业车间，存在由混凝土养护、防水测试、油漆干燥等导致的长时间后操作时滞，这会使最优参考完工时间平均膨胀约67%。忽略这些时滞进行调度后再修复不可行，效果甚至差于任何调度规则，因此亟需一种能感知时滞的高效调度方法。

Method: 本文对最先进的双注意力深度强化学习求解器进行三项最小侵入式扩展：（1）时滞感知动力学与可接受奖励界限；（2）两个前瞻性时滞特征通道；（3）活性屏蔽的操作类型与工位类型嵌入。所有扩展可单独消融，禁用时完全复现原求解器。

Result: 在基于官方国家预制化指南的基准实例上，所提方法与约束规划基准差距约4%，优于所有调度规则和遗传算法元启发式。优势在容量竞争加剧时更明显，单一混合规模策略在训练范围内均保持领先。方法无需求解器、模型或许可证，可在数秒内完成中断重规划。

Conclusion: 所提时滞感知深度强化学习方法在无需精确求解器的情况下成为最强的无求解器调度方案，精确求解器仍是质量上限，本文明确刻画了这一边界。

Abstract: Prefabricated prefinished volumetric construction moves most building work into module factories, whose production floor operates as a flexible job shop. A major complication is decisive: long post-operation time-lags caused by concrete curing, watertightness ponding tests, and paint drying, during which a module is blocked while its workstation stays free. On benchmark instances grounded in an official national prefabrication guidebook, these lags inflate even the optimal reference makespan by about 67% on average, and ignoring them at decision time, then repairing to feasibility, is worse than every dispatching rule. We adapt a state-of-the-art dual-attention deep reinforcement learning solver through three minimally invasive, individually ablatable extensions: lag-aware dynamics with an admissible reward bound, two anticipatory lag feature channels, and liveness-masked operation- and station-type embeddings. With every extension disabled the implementation reproduces the original solver exactly, so all gains are attributable to the adaptations. We release a public, guidebook-grounded benchmark generator. On held-out instances the learned policy is the strongest solver-free scheduler: it reaches within about 4% of a constraint-programming reference and beats every dispatching rule and a genetic-algorithm metaheuristic, with its advantage widening under capacity contention, and a single size-mixed policy carries this lead across the trained range of factory sizes. It needs no solver, model, or license in the loop and re-plans within seconds of a disruption; where an exact solver can be deployed, that solver remains the quality ceiling, a boundary we map explicitly.

</details>


### [162] [HiFi-LLP: High-Fidelity, Low-Cost Latency Predictors with Confidence for Robust HW-NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11746)
*Shambhavi Balamuthu Sampath,Behzad Shomali,Nael Fasfous,Moritz Thoma,Judeson Anthony Fernando,Lukas Frickenstein,Pierpaolo Mori,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Alexander Frickenstein,Walter Stechele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiFi-LLP，一种基于图注意力网络的高保真低代价延迟预测器，结合置信度指标，用于替代硬件在环（HIL）测量以加速硬件感知神经网络架构搜索（HW-NAS）。HiFi-LLP在LatBench六种设备上Spearman秩相关达0.996，并提出混合NAS框架，将低置信度预测路由至HIL，实现最高8.6倍加速同时保持竞争性帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件感知优化技术（如HW-aware压缩和HW-NAS）依赖实时硬件反馈（HIL）进行延迟测量，但HIL的顺序化特性成为并行化搜索的瓶颈。现有延迟预测器方法面临两大挑战：平台特定预测器需要数万样本训练，且不准确预测会误导NAS过程。因此需要一种高保真、低成本的延迟预测方法。

Method: 提出HiFi-LLP，基于图注意力网络（GAT）构建延迟预测器，并引入置信度度量机制。进一步设计混合NAS框架：对高置信度预测直接使用HiFi-LLP结果，对低置信度预测回退至HIL测量，实现预测精度与测量效率的平衡。

Result: HiFi-LLP在10%准确率边界上比先前平台特定预测器提升最多9个百分点，在LatBench六种设备上Spearman秩相关达到0.996。混合NAS框架相比典型NAS实现最高8.6倍加速，同时保持竞争性的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 提出高保真低代价延迟预测器HiFi-LLP，有效解决HW-NAS中HIL测量瓶颈问题，结合置信度路由的混合框架在加速搜索的同时不牺牲模型质量。

Abstract: With deep neural networks (DNNs) increasingly deployed on edge devices, hardware (HW)-aware optimization techniques--such as HW-aware compression and HW-aware neural architecture search (HW-NAS)--have become essential. These methods rely on real feedback from the target hardware to tailor DNN architectures for efficient deployment. While the search can be parallelized, latency measurements via hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) remain a bottleneck due to their sequential nature. Recent approaches use latency predictors to replace costly HIL feedback, but challenges persist: (1) platform-specific predictors often require tens of thousands of samples, and (2) inaccurate predictions can mislead the NAS process. To address this, we introduce HiFi-LLP, a high-fidelity, low-cost latency predictor based on graph attention networks, augmented with a confidence metric. HiFi-LLP outperforms prior platform-specific predictors by up to 9 percentage points (p.p.) in the 10% accuracy bound and achieves a Spearman's rank correlation of up to 0.996 across six devices in the LatBench dataset. We further propose a hybrid NAS framework that routes low-confidence predictions to HIL, achieving up to 8.6$\times$ speedup compared to typical NAS while maintaining a competitive Pareto front.

</details>


### [163] [From Global to Factor-Wise Expert Composition in Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11758)
*Haozhe Huang,Yudong Xu,Abhijoy Mandal,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FactorDiff提出了一种面向扩散模型的因子级组合框架，通过对样本进行细粒度分解并为每个因子动态路由到最相关的专家，优于传统的全局标量加权方法。在ARC-AGI基准上实验表明，简单的因子特定路由在需要逻辑一致性和空间解耦的任务上持续超越复杂全局加权方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的组合生成方法采用逐样本处理的方式，将每个生成状态视为整体，忽略了不同专家在空间或功能上的专门化潜力，无法充分利用专家模型的局部优势。

Method: 提出FactorDiff框架，将样本分解为更小的因子（如像素级空间因子），在采样过程中为每个因子动态路由到最适合的专家模型，实现细粒度的因子级组合。

Result: 在ARC-AGI基准上，简单的因子特定路由方法在需要逻辑一致性和空间解耦的任务中，持续优于复杂的全局标量加权方案。

Conclusion: 通过因子分解和动态路由，能够更有效地利用多个预训练专家的专门化能力，提升复杂推理任务中的生成质量和组合泛化能力。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a powerful framework for solving complex reasoning tasks, particularly through compositional generation, which combines multiple pre-trained experts to generalize beyond their individual training data. Recent theoretical corrections introduce time-dependent mixing weights to better align composed diffusion dynamics with the intended target. However, these methods are fundamentally limited by working on a per-sample basis, treating each generated state monolithically and ignoring the potential spatial or functional specializations of different experts. In this work, we address this limitation by proposing FactorDiff - a factor-wise composition framework for diffusion models. We posit that samples can be further decomposed into smaller factors, and propose a sampling process that dynamically routes each factor to the most relevant expert. We instantiate this framework with spatial/pixel-level compositions and validate it on the ARC-AGI benchmark, demonstrating that simple factor-specific routing consistently outperforms complex global scalar weighting schemes on tasks that require logical consistency and spatial disentanglement.

</details>


### [164] [From Expressivity to Sample Complexity: Narrow Teachers for Transformers via C-RASP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11760)
*Michael Rizvi-Martel,Satwik Bhattamishra,Guillaume Rabusseau,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer模型的可学习性（而非仅表达性）展开理论分析，基于损失景观分析，首次提出了学习C-RASP构造的样本复杂度界限，填补了Transformer可学习性研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大量工作通过手工构造权重或计算复杂性论证分析了Transformer的表达性（哪些任务在假设类中），但很少有工作研究这些解的可学习性。本文旨在推进对Transformer可学习性的理论理解。

Method: 受近期损失景观分析工作的启发，提出了学习C-RASP构造的样本复杂度界限。

Result: 初步提出了Transformer学习C-RASP程序的样本复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 本文首次从可学习性角度分析Transformer，为理解LLM的理论能力与局限提供了新视角，但属于初步探索。

Abstract: A theoretical understanding of Transformers is crucial to better understand the capacities and limitations of large language models (LLMs). There is much work analyzing the expressivity of attention-based models. By proposing handcrafted weights or using computational complexity arguments, a large amount of past theoretical works have sought to characterize which tasks are and which are not in the hypothesis class of Transformer models. However, little work investigates the learnability of such solutions. In this work, we make progress towards this goal. Inspired by recent loss landscape analysis work, we propose preliminary sample complexity bounds for learning C-RASP constructions with Transformers.

</details>


### [165] [An Exact Instrument for State Usage in Selective State-Space Models, and the Input-Driven Migration It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11796)
*Raktim Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确测量选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba）中模态使用情况的工具，发现模型会根据输入动态重新分配状态空间，其中输入相关的写入映射B_t是模态迁移的主要原因。基于此的输入调度模态剪枝在多种规模模型上优于静态方法，在半状态预算下达到未剪枝模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练后的选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba）如何利用其模态（模式），以及模态使用是否随输入动态变化，从而为模型压缩和优化提供理论依据。

Method: 1. 利用对角状态矩阵的特性，将每个通道的输出精确分解为每个模态的贡献；2. 构建每（层、通道、窗口）的Gram张量，用于离线计算任意子集模态丢弃的精确输出误差；3. 通过冻结信号的反事实分析，识别模态迁移的主要驱动因素；4. 提出基于输入调度的模态剪枝方法，先通过第一遍前向计算获得窗口级模态使用情况，再据此进行剪枝。

Result: 验证实验中与参考实现相对误差2.3×10⁻⁷，预测剪枝误差中位数相对偏差5×10⁻⁷。发现训练模型会根据输入动态重新分配状态空间：携带信号的模态在不同上下文中迁移，最受影响的层中每个输入的oracle剪枝可使误差减半。输入调度剪枝在130M到7B各规模上均优于静态、Hankel基和层自适应方法，半状态预算下匹配未剪枝模型性能。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间模型中的模态使用是输入相关的，主要由B_t驱动而非时间步长。基于此发现的输入调度模态剪枝策略能有效利用模态迁移特性，在剪枝时保持模型性能，证明了模型存在可实现的优化空间。

Abstract: Selective state-space models such as Mamba route information through a bank of first-order modes whose input coupling is set by a learned selection mechanism. We give an exact instrument for measuring how a trained model uses these modes. Because the state matrix is diagonal, each channel's output decomposes exactly into per-mode contributions, and a per-(layer, channel, window) Gram tensor yields the exact output error of dropping any subset of modes, offline, at any budget. Validated against the reference implementation to a relative error of $2.3\times10^{-7}$ on the Mamba-1 family where it is exact, the instrument predicts a layer's deployed pruning error to a median relative deviation of $5\times10^{-7}$ over $4{,}464$ configurations, its floor set by the reconstruction. Applying the instrument across the Mamba-1 family (130M--2.8B), the deployed 7B Falcon-Mamba, and Mamba-2, we find that trained models re-allocate their state space with the input: which modes carry the signal migrates across contexts, and at the most affected layers a per-input oracle roughly halves the output error of a fixed mode set. Frozen-signal counterfactuals attribute the migration primarily to the input-dependent write map $B_t$; the timestep usually identified with selectivity carries almost none of it. Input-scheduled mode pruning on this measurement outperforms static, Hankel-based, and layer-adaptive rankings at every scale from 130M to the deployed 7B Falcon-Mamba, and at half the state budget it matches the unpruned model. Because the scheduler reads each window's mode usage from a first pass, this demonstrates realizable headroom; we claim no deployed compute or memory saving.

</details>


### [166] [Relaxing Faithfulness with Intervention-Only Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11816)
*Bijan Mazaheri,Jiaqi Zhang,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出干预-即时忠实性假设，替代传统忠实性假设，证明硬干预能携带因果结构信息，从而在允许路径抵消（违反忠实性）的情况下非参数识别因果结构，建议将干预作为因果发现的主要信息载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果发现算法依赖忠实性假设，但许多自然系统存在缓冲和稳定路径的抵消效应，违反该假设，导致算法错误移除因果依赖关系。

Method: 提出干预-即时忠实性（intervention-immediacy faithfulness）这一较弱假设，允许路径抵消，并证明在此假设下利用硬干预可非参数识别因果结构；同时给出未满足识别条件时的等价类。

Result: 硬干预包含被第一阶段条件独立性测试忽略的因果连接信息；在干预-即时忠实性假设下，因果结构可被非参数识别。

Conclusion: 应翻转范式：将干预作为因果结构信息的主要载体，优先于条件独立性测试。

Abstract: Causal discovery algorithms learn a network that describes the causal dependencies among random variables. A common workflow involves first utilizing conditional independence properties on observational data to determine partially directed causal relationships, then applying interventions to orient the unknown causal directions. A critical assumption for the first step is faithfulness: a requirement that causally linked variables exhibit statistical dependence. Many natural systems include buffering and stabilizing pathways that cancel out to achieve systemic robustness. This cancellation of pathways violates faithfulness, leading causal discovery algorithms to incorrectly remove causal dependencies. In this paper, we argue that hard interventions contain information about the presence/absence of causal linkage that is overlooked in the first stage of structure discovery. We show that a mild assumption -- called intervention-immediacy faithfulness -- that allows cancellations, is sufficient to nonparametrically identify causal structures with hard interventions. These results position interventions as the primary carriers of information about causal structure, which should take precedence over conditional independence testing. To flip the paradigm, we also specify equivalence classes when the identification criteria are not met due to limitations in the scope of interventions.

</details>


### [167] [Transformer-Guided Swarm Intelligence for Frugal Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11826)
*Romain Amigon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种节俭的memetic神经架构搜索框架，结合自回归Transformer控制器（强化学习）与人工蜂群算法，在消费级GPU（RTX 3060）上3小时内即可搜索到高效架构，在CIFAR-10上以约17.4万参数达到84.85%精度，在信用卡欺诈检测上以约4600参数实现0.71的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NAS需要大量计算资源（数千GPU天），阻碍了其在普通硬件上的应用。本文旨在通过提出一种节俭的memetic NAS框架，在消费级硬件上实现自动架构设计，降低NAS的使用门槛。

Method: 提出一种memetic混合框架：1）全局宏观搜索由基于强化学习训练的自回归Transformer控制器负责；2）局部微观利用由人工蜂群算法负责；3）引入动态熵机制，在RL阶段检测到性能停滞时强制进行拓扑探索以防止早熟收敛；4）通过算法惩罚网络深度来抑制模型膨胀。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 3060上，CIFAR-10数据集达到84.85%精度，仅约17.4万参数（显著小于ResNet-20等基线），搜索时间3小时；信用卡欺诈检测任务达到0.71的F1分数，仅约4600参数。

Conclusion: 该方法能够生成定制化、可访问且参数高效适用于边缘部署的深度学习模型，有效解决了元启发式方法的冷启动问题，展示了在平衡数据和高度不平衡表格数据上的通用性。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has automated the design of deep learning models but traditionally requires massive computational resources, often measured in thousands of GPU-days. In this paper, we propose a frugal and memetic NAS framework designed to democratize architecture design on consumer-grade hardware. Our approach combines the global macro-search capabilities of an autoregressive Transformer controller, trained via Reinforcement Learning (RL), with the local micro-exploitation of an Artificial Bee Colony (ABC) algorithm. To prevent premature convergence during the RL phase, we introduce a dynamic entropy mechanism that forces topological exploration upon detection of performance stagnation. Evaluated on a standard GPU (NVIDIA RTX 3060), our hybrid method effectively resolves the "cold-start" problem inherent in metaheuristics. By algorithmically penalizing network depth, our framework actively mitigates model bloat: on the CIFAR-10 dataset, it discovers an efficient architecture reaching 84.85% accuracy with only $\sim$174,000 parameters (significantly smaller than standard baselines like ResNet-20) in 3 hours of search time. Furthermore, we demonstrate the framework's flexibility by applying it to credit card fraud detection, directly optimizing the F1-Score on highly imbalanced tabular data to reach a F1-Score of 0.71 with a compact network of $\sim$4,600 parameters. These results suggest that our approach can yield tailored, accessible, and highly parameter-efficient deep learning models suitable for edge deployment.

</details>


### [168] [Inside the Unfair Judge: A Mechanistic Interpretability Account of LLM-as-Judge Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11871)
*Zixiang Xu,Sixian Li,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有LLM-as-judge评分偏差研究主要停留在输入-输出层面，本文从表征层面揭示了偏差在隐状态中的几何结构，实现了因果控制和操作预测的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏差研究局限于输入-输出层面的扰动与修正，缺乏对偏差内部表征机制的理解。本文旨在从隐状态表征层面解释偏差的几何结构，并探索其因果控制与操作预测能力。

Method: 在7个裁判模型、7种偏差类型、9个基准上进行了三方面研究：(1)几何分析：考察基线输入与偏差输入的激活流形差异，发现偏差沿低维子空间位移；(2)因果控制：沿偏差子空间进行隐状态操控，正向/反向驱动评分变化；(3)操作预测：通过线性投影到偏差方向特征，预测裁判在未见基准上的失败表现。

Result: (1)偏差输入沿低维、类型特异性子空间位移，该子空间随层深锐化；(2)沿该子空间操控隐状态可双向驱动评分，效果比随机方向大一个数量级；(3)线性投影到偏差方向特征对未见基准的预测显著优于基于文本的方法。

Conclusion: 将偏差视为激活几何而非输入-输出噪声，统一了几何结构、因果控制和操作预测三个层面，为理解和缓解LLM评分偏差提供了新的表征框架。

Abstract: Existing studies of LLM-as-judge scoring bias work predominantly at the input-output level: they perturb inputs, measure score deltas, and propose prompt-level mitigations. We argue that the same biases admit a representation-level account in the judge's hidden state, complementary to the input-output view and operationally useful in ways it does not afford. We report three findings, across seven judges, seven bias types, and nine benchmarks. Geometry: baseline judging inputs occupy a tight activation manifold while biased inputs are displaced along a low-dimensional, type-specific subspace that sharpens with depth and is recovered consistently by three families of estimators. Causal control: steering hidden states along this subspace drives scoring in both directions, forward shifts reproducing biased scoring on clean inputs and reverse shifts restoring baseline scoring on biased ones, while matched-norm random directions produce shifts an order of magnitude smaller. Operational: a simple linear projection onto the same bias-direction features anticipates judge failures on three entirely unseen benchmarks, substantially outperforming text-based alternatives. Reading bias as activation geometry, rather than as input-output noise, unifies geometric structure, causal control, and operational prediction within a single framework. The project page is available at https://xzx34.github.io/unfair-judge/

</details>


### [169] [Invariant Learning Dynamics of Transformers in Inductive Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11875)
*Tiberiu Musat,Tiago Pimentel,Nicholas Zucchet,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论框架，解释Transformer语言模型中归纳推理能力的涌现机制。通过将训练动态约束在低维不变流形上，用少数可解释坐标而非百万参数刻画学习过程，揭示了数据统计如何影响上下文学习与权重内学习的竞争、随机初始化如何决定胜出电路，并利用该流形自动检测已学习的电路结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于Transformer学习动态的研究大多局限于特定任务，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解归纳推理能力的涌现机制。

Method: 定义了一类广义归纳任务（统一了上下文n-gram和多跳推理等合成任务），理论证明注意力模型训练动态可被约束在低维不变流形上，用少量可解释坐标而非百万参数刻画学习动态。

Result: 1) 刻画了数据统计如何支配上下文学习与权重内学习的竞争；2) 揭示了随机初始化如何决定多解情境下的"胜出"电路；3) 证明了该流形坐标可用于自动检测已学习的电路结构。

Conclusion: 将电路形成建模为低维动态现象，朝着建立Transformer学习的可预测理论迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework to explain the emergence of inductive reasoning abilities in Transformer language models. While previous works on Transformer learning dynamics have so far been mostly tied to specific tasks, we study a generalized class of inductive tasks that unifies several synthetic tasks known in the literature, including in-context n-grams and multi-hop reasoning. In this class, we theoretically prove that the training dynamics of attention models can be confined to a highly interpretable, low-dimensional invariant manifold. On this manifold, the learning dynamics are captured by a handful of interpretable coordinates rather than millions of parameters, making both theoretical and empirical analysis more tractable. Using this framework, we characterize how data statistics govern the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, we study how random initializations determine the `winning' circuit when multiple solutions are possible, and we demonstrate that the coordinate frame associated with the manifold can be used to automatically detect which circuits have been learned in trained models. By casting circuit formation as a low-dimensional dynamical phenomenon, we take a step toward a predictive theory of how Transformers learn.

</details>


### [170] [Requential Coding: Pushing the Limits of Model Compression with Self-Generated Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11883)
*Shikai Qiu,Marc Finzi,Yujia Zheng,Kun Zhang,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**requential coding**方法，通过教师模型从学生自身分布中选择训练样本进行压缩，编码长度与参数量和数据熵无关，在LLM上比现有方法压缩数个数量级，并给出当前最优的泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法（基于参数的量化、基于轨迹的前缀编码）存在编码长度随模型规模或数据熵增长的问题，无法真正反映模型学到的简单性。需要一种编码长度不受参数量和原始数据熵影响的压缩方法，以揭示大规模模型的泛化本质。

Method: 提出**requential coding**：教师模型从学生自身分布中选取训练样本，学生仅编码这些选择结果。仅在教师与学生预测不一致处产生编码代价，从而码长独立于参数量和原始数据熵。

Result: 1) 码长比前缀编码少数个数量级，优势随规模增长而扩大；2) 在固定损失下，更大模型和集成模型压缩后更小；3) 接入PAC-Bayes界后，对十亿参数LLM给出当前最优泛化保证，超过基于量化方法（即使量化误差为零）；4) 在计算最优尺度下，界随规模增大而收紧；5) 预测多轮训练时模型逐渐过拟合；6) 分离数据中可学习信息与不可预测随机内容，发现低熵文本比高熵图像含有更多可学习结构。

Conclusion: Requential coding是一种更优的模型压缩度量方式，能揭示模型真正捕获的简单性，为理解大模型的泛化、过拟合和数据中的可学习结构提供新视角，并在理论保证上取得最先进结果。

Abstract: Compression is fundamental to intelligence. A model that can represent its training data as a short code has discovered regularities that enable generalization. Large neural networks may learn functions far simpler than their parameter counts suggest, but it is challenging to construct codes that realize this simplicity. Parameter-based methods such as quantization produce code lengths that scale with model size, insensitive to how much information the parameters store. Prequential coding bypasses this issue by compressing the training trajectory, but codes the exact data sequence regardless of how much the model learns, yielding large codes when the data has high entropy. We introduce requential coding, where a teacher model selects training samples drawn from the student's own distribution. The student's code records only these selections, which cost bits only where teacher and student disagree. The resulting code length is independent of parameter count and data entropy, and often orders of magnitude shorter than the prequential counterpart, with an advantage that grows with scale. This compression sheds light on phenomena inaccessible to prior compressors. Holding loss fixed, larger models and ensembles compress to much smaller sizes despite more parameters. Plugged into a PAC-Bayes bound, the requential code yields state-of-the-art generalization guarantees for billion-parameter LLMs, outperforming bounds built on aggressive post-training quantization even granted zero error. The bound tightens with scale in the compute-optimal regime, as models become increasingly compressible relative to dataset size. The same code predicts that models gradually overfit when trained for multiple epochs. It also isolates the learnable information in a dataset from its unpredictable, random content, revealing that lower-entropy text holds far more learnable structure than higher-entropy image data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [A Note on Diameter Certification in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09929)
*Josef Erik Sedláček*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对树的直径问题，提出了一种1-局部认证方案，该方案使用最多3⌈log₂(d+1)⌉比特的证书来认证给定树的直径是否不超过d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部认证模型中，通用图的直径认证需要大尺寸证书，而树结构允许更高效的解决方案。本文旨在为树的直径提供一种高效的局部认证方案。

Method: 提出了一种1-局部认证方案，通过构造不超过3⌈log₂(d+1)⌉比特的证书来验证树的直径是否至多为d。

Result: 实现了对树直径的1-局部认证，证书大小仅为3⌈log₂(d+1)⌉比特。

Conclusion: 该方案证明了树结构在直径局部认证问题上比通用图具有显著效率优势，提供了紧凑的证书规模上界。

Abstract: In the local certification model, certifying the diameter of general graphs requires large certificates, but trees admit more efficient solutions. In this note, we provide a $1$-local certification scheme that certifies whether the diameter of a given tree is at most $d$ using certificates of at most $3\lceil\log_2(d+1)\rceil$ bits.

</details>


### [172] [Trusted Floors Under Untrusted Learners: A Runtime Assured-SLO Guard for ML Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09992)
*Hsiu-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在ML推理系统中，用一个轻量级可信守卫（Guard）包裹不可信的学习型组件（如准入器、路由器），将租户的SLO义务拆分为“安全投影”（可由守卫运行时强制执行）和“聚合义务”（统计残差，无逐请求 enforcement 点）。实验表明守卫能在学习组件任意错误下保证0违反率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML服务中，学习型组件（路由器、延迟SLO准入器）可能做出错误决策，导致SLO无声违反；而Kubernetes下层（Kueue、DRA、GAIE）又引入跨层不确定性。现有方法缺乏对学习组件犯错时保证SLO不违约的机制。

Method: 提出"Learned Proposes, Guard Disposes"框架：将租户SLO拆分为（1）安全投影——每请求可行性+每类每窗口服务下限，由守卫通过预留和优先级调度在运行时强制执行；（2）聚合义务——尾延迟百分位，为统计残差，无逐请求强制点。守卫包络不可信学习组件，无论其如何错误，都能保证安全投影不违约。同时分析廉价确定性筛选器在何时可信、何时不可信。

Result: 在真实2xV100上，守卫在两种失败方向相反的丢弃策略下，面对学习型准入器的各种错误校准，保证保证类违反率为0.0（10次重复，Wilson CI上限0.0053）。在GAIE Flow Control模拟器中，错误标注的路由器使保证类违反率从0.0升至1.0；守卫按真实类别预留，标签无法破坏服务下限。

Conclusion: 守卫机制能有效约束不可信学习型组件带来的SLO风险，将安全投影作为可控制义务强制执行。廉价静态筛选器在非饱和区乐观但不可靠，需谨慎使用。作者将该工作定位为Frontiers-style提交，评估基于商用2xV100和服务模拟器，并将数据中心规模、真实模型Flow Control、封闭最坏情况定理作为未来议程。

Abstract: Modern ML serving increasingly lets learned, unbounded components (routers, latency-SLO admitters, admit ladders) decide a tenant's quality of service; when one is wrong, the assured SLO can silently break, and the Kubernetes layers beneath (Kueue, DRA, the Gateway-API Inference Extension) only add cross-layer surprises. Rather than trust the learner to be right, we bound the damage a wrong one can do: a small trusted guard wraps the untrusted learner -- learned proposes, the guard disposes. A tenant's assured-SLO obligation splits into two parts with different epistemics. Its safety projection (per-request dispatch feasibility and a per-class, per-window service floor) is a controllable obligation the guard enforces at runtime, regardless of an arbitrarily-wrong learned admitter, by reservation and priority dispatch. Its aggregate obligation (a tail-latency percentile) is a statistical residual with no per-request enforcement point; a cheap deterministic screen for it is optimistically unsound not only near saturation but well below it. On real 2xV100 the guard holds assured-class miss 0.0 (reps 10, worst upper Wilson CI 0.0053) across every miscalibration of a learned admitter under two shed policies that fail in opposite directions. Against a real, deployed GAIE Flow Control on a serving simulator, a mislabelling router flips the same assured requests from miss 0.0 to 1.0; our guard reserves by the true class, so the label cannot break its floor. We characterize when a cheap static screen can be trusted and when it cannot. As a Frontiers-style submission we evaluate the stance on commodity 2xV100 and a serving simulator, and scope datacenter scale, real-model Flow Control, and a closed worst-case theorem as the agenda.

</details>


### [173] [Descriptive Execution of HPC Applications and Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10081)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个结合大语言模型和工具资源的自主智能体框架在高性能计算中的三项任务表现：优化运行AWS上的HPC扩展研究、高精度转换Slurm与Flux作业规范、以及设计执行生物信息学工作流。智能体成功完成所有任务，但暴露出特定失败模式，如低效的作业监控、处理器亲和性标志转换错误等。在生物信息学工作流中，智能体几乎完美复现了专家编写的变异检测流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC领域正从人工编写代码转向描述性文本定义任务，大语言模型与工具资源的结合催生了自主智能体框架。为了在科学计算中安全可靠地部署这类全自动化和智能体框架，必须针对特定任务评估其可靠性并识别策略局限。

Method: 构建一个结合大语言模型与工具函数/资源的自主智能体框架，设计三项HPC基准任务进行实证评估：1) 在AWS低延迟网络上优化运行HPC扩展研究；2) 在Slurm与Flux工作负载管理器间高精度转换作业规范；3) 设计并运行完整的生物信息学工作流（变异检测管线）。记录任务完成情况并分析失败模式。

Result: 框架成功完成全部三项任务，但暴露了任务特定的失败模式：扩展研究中智能体偏好保守的固定等待而非事件订阅，导致作业监控效率低下；作业转换中处理器亲和性标志是最常见错误；生物信息学工作流中智能体在19次运行中有18次与专家参考调用集一致，并通过多种功能等效的实现路径达到该结果。

Conclusion: 自主智能体框架在处理HPC任务时具备可行性，但需要针对任务特定的失败模式进行优化。这些发现对于未来开发由智能体处理调度和转换的多集群HPC系统具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: The means to execute and orchestrate software components has changed from human-written code to descriptive prose. In high performance computing, this transition is represented in application orchestration, workload management, and system monitoring and debugging, to name a few. The underlying means to enable descriptive definition of tasks is the use of the Large Language Model with associated tool functions and resources. A combination of a model with access to such resources, modeled in software, encompasses an autonomous framework. As fully automated and agentic frameworks are developed for science, it is important to assess reliability and strategies scoped to specific tasks. In this work, we assess the extent to which an agentic framework can optimize and run an HPC scaling study with a low latency network in Amazon Web Services, accurately transform HPC job specifications between workload managers, and design and run an entire biosciences workflow. We find that the framework completes all three tasks while surfacing task-specific failure modes. In the scaling study, agents deploy and optimize applications but monitor running jobs inefficiently, preferring conservative fixed waits over event subscriptions. In job translation, they convert specifications between Slurm and Flux with high accuracy, with processor-affinity flags the most common error. In the bioscience workflow, the agent reproduces an expert-written variant-calling pipeline almost exactly -- agreeing with the reference call set in 18 of 19 completed runs -- and reaches this result through many distinct yet functionally equivalent workflow implementations. This information is invaluable moving forward to developing multi-cluster setups with scheduling and transformation handled by agents.

</details>


### [174] [Consensus as Collapse Policy: Communication Evidence, Horizons, and Prefix Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10152)
*Tomislav Grospić*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将共识协议重新解读为"基于证据的坍缩策略"：分布式执行产生通信证据（二阶状态），共识输出（如决策值）是该证据的一阶投影。协议被比较为不同的坍缩策略——规定何种证据合法、检查的时间范围、何时将证据投影为输出，以及在证据不足时如何修复或推迟坍缩。FLP等不可能性结果也被纳入该框架重新诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识协议通常只关注最终输出（决策值、复制日志等），忽略了使输出安全的通信证据。本文旨在显式化这个被隐藏的"证据载体"，提供一个统一的、基于证据坍缩的规约框架来比较和理解不同共识协议。

Method: 采用范畴论/拓扑视角：将分布式执行建模为由通信诱导的序2证据状态，共识输出作为该状态的序1投影。通过"坍缩策略"的概念统一描述不同协议——规定合法证据、检查时间范围、投影时机及修复机制。并将FLP、Set agreement等经典不可能性结果纳入该框架重新诠释。

Result: 提出了一个称为"共识即证据坍缩"的指称规约框架，能够统一描述和比较不同共识协议，并一致地容纳经典不可能性结果（FLP、Set agreement、拓扑分布式计算）的区分。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不在于新的不可能性定理或替代协议级证明，而在于提供了一个更高层次的、指称性的规约框架，将共识统一理解为"基于证据的坍缩"。

Abstract: Consensus protocols are usually specified by their terminal artifact: a decided value, replicated log, or finalized prefix. This output-first view hides the communication-derived evidence that makes such artifacts safe. This paper makes that carrier explicit: distributed execution is read as an order-2 evidence state induced by communication, while classical consensus outputs are order-1 projections of that state.
  Under this view, consensus protocols can be compared as collapse policies. A protocol specifies which evidence is legitimate, which finite horizon it inspects, when it projects communication evidence into a value or prefix, and how it repairs or defers collapse when the visible evidence is insufficient. The impossibility lineage supports the same distinction. FLP is not a statement that communication cannot accumulate structure; it constrains deterministic guaranteed collapse to a terminal decision under full asynchrony with one crash failure. Set agreement then exposes the width of the output carrier, and topological distributed computing asks when a history/view carrier admits a structure-preserving map to an output carrier. The contribution is not a new impossibility theorem or a replacement for protocol-specific proofs, but a denotational specification framework: consensus is collapse under evidence.

</details>


### [175] [Automated Tensor Scheduling for Hybrid CPU-GPU LLM Inference on Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10183)
*Yangyijian Liu,Hongyi Ye,Mingyang Li,Wu-jun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ATSInfer 是一种面向消费级设备的混合 CPU-GPU 推理系统，通过张量级卸载（tensor-granularity offloading）结合静态放置与负载感知的动态传输，以及异步 CPU-GPU 协调调度，显著提升本地大模型推理的 prefill 吞吐量（最高 1.94 倍）和 decode 吞吐量（最高 3.29 倍），改善 GPU 利用率和 PCIe 带宽效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费设备（笔记本、台式机）上运行大语言模型时，模型权重常超出 GPU 显存容量，需要借助 CPU 内存进行卸载推理。现有卸载系统采用粗粒度的层级或专家级调度，忽略了同一层内张量间的异构性，且对设备上变化的硬件负载条件适应能力差。

Method: 提出 ATSInfer 系统：1）采用张量粒度卸载，实现比层级/专家级更细粒度的调度；2）结合静态张量放置与负载感知的动态传输；3）引入异步 CPU-GPU 协调机制，在异构后端间高效调度存储、数据移动和计算。

Result: 在消费级平台上对稠密模型和 MoE 模型进行评估：相比现有系统，prefill 吞吐量提升最高 1.94 倍，decode 吞吐量提升最高 3.29 倍，同时 GPU 利用率和 PCIe 带宽利用率均得到改善。

Conclusion: ATSInfer 能显著提升个人消费设备上本地部署 LLM 的用户体验。

Abstract: Running large language models on consumer devices such as laptops and desktops is challenging because model weights often exceed GPU memory capacity, making offloading inference necessary to extend effective model capacity with CPU memory. Existing offloading systems, however, typically rely on coarse layer-level or expert-level scheduling, which overlooks substantial heterogeneity among tensors within the same layer and adapts poorly to changing hardware load conditions on such devices. This paper presents ATSInfer, a hybrid CPU-GPU inference system for consumer devices that performs offloading at tensor granularity. ATSInfer combines static tensor placement with load-aware dynamic transfer, and introduces asynchronous CPU-GPU coordination to efficiently schedule hardware storage, data movement, and computation across heterogeneous backends. We implement ATSInfer and evaluate it on representative consumer platforms using both dense and MoE models. Compared with existing systems, ATSInfer improves prefill throughput by up to 1.94$\times$ and decode throughput by up to 3.29$\times$, while also increasing GPU utilization and making more effective use of PCIe bandwidth. These results show that ATSInfer can substantially improve the user experience of local LLM deployment on personal consumer devices.

</details>


### [176] [Byzantine Accountability Without Consensus: Strong Eventual Consistency for Non-Associative, Stochastic, Robust Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10305)
*Ryan Gillespie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ACFA（可问责无共识聚合）框架，通过CRDT副本的确定性纯函数实现拜占庭鲁棒聚合，无需中心协调器或共识协议，在强最终一致性下保证拜占庭容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则（如multi-Krum）假设存在中心协调器，而将其去中心化面临根本性障碍：这些规则是全局耦合、非结合且不连续的，微小的扰动即可翻转所选子集。因此需要一种无协调器复制的鲁棒聚合方法。

Method: ACFA复制了一个内容寻址的OR-Set（存储带签名的贡献）和一个仅增长的自我认证的等权证明集合。聚合是一个关于收敛产物状态的确定性纯函数：基于哈希规范顺序的定点整数算术，平局由内容哈希决定。证明任何CRDT收敛产物的纯函数（无论非单调、非结合还是随机的）都继承强最终一致性及其逆命题。

Result: 原型系统（10节点，3个拜占庭节点）通过了16/16验证检查：包括对抗性八卦下的字节一致根、延迟等权证明后的确定性重新收敛、分区恢复以及三个破坏字节一致性的消融实验。

Conclusion: ACFA提供的保证是一致性而非准确性；鲁棒性是有条件的，依赖于2f+3个被接受的贡献（最多f个拜占庭节点）和指定的量化边界条件。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust aggregation rules such as multi-Krum assume a central coordinator, and decentralising them is obstructed by the rules themselves: they are globally coupled, non-associative, and discontinuous, so an ulpscale perturbation can flip the selected subset, moving the output by a non-vanishing amount. None of this prevents coordinator-free replication, because a robust rule needs no agreed order of contributions, only an agreed set and an agreed exclusion predicate, both of which converge without consensus. ACFA (Accountable Consensus-Free Aggregation) replicates a content-addressed OR-Set of signed contributions and a grow-only set of self-authenticating equivocation proofs, offline-verifiable by anyone. Aggregation is a deterministic pure function of the converged product state: fixed-point integer arithmetic over a hash-canonical order, ties broken by content hash. We prove that any pure function of a converged product of CRDTs (non-monotone, non-associative, or stochastic) inherits Strong Eventual Consistency, together with its converse; the contribution is the composition of a data lattice with an evidence lattice applied to a robust selector, not the elementary lifting step. A prototype (10 nodes, 3 Byzantine) passes 16/16 falsification checks: byte-identical roots under adversarial gossip, deterministic re-convergence after late equivocation proofs, partition recovery, and three byte-identity-breaking ablations. The guarantee is consistency, not accuracy; robustness is imported, conditional on 2f + 3 admitted contributions (at most f Byzantine) and a stated quantisation-margin condition.

</details>


### [177] [Stateful Worlds, Stateless Elasticity: Exact-State Serving for Interactive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10389)
*Jin Li,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldMove系统，实现了在GPU间实时迁移大型交互式世界模型的注意力缓存（多GB级别），保证目的端与源端完全比特一致，迁移速度达18.8ms（同节点），比传统保存/加载快101倍，并在100G网络上维持92.1-94.8 Gb/s吞吐。系统包含可接受性条件、incast感知的准入控制器和无损GPU编解码器，以解决调度、传输和验证的联合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久交互式世界模型需要在GPU上维护运行状态（多GB注意力缓存），每次生成步骤几乎全部重写。该状态无法在交互时间内重新计算或近似而不改变世界，因此实时会话会固定其所在设备，形成了调度难题。现有方法无法在保证比特一致性的前提下高效迁移活会话状态。

Method: 提出WorldMove系统，核心方法包括：(1) 同节点18.8ms内迁移缓存，比保存/加载快101倍；(2) 校验和验证确保92.1-94.8 Gb/s吞吐（100G网络）；(3) 主动生成会话在块边界收敛，目的端继续逐比特运行；(4) 可接受性条件决定每次迁移：移动必须在读取视界内完成，带宽覆盖状态及其脏速率；(5) 提升为集群可调度性测试，指导合并循环执行48/48次跨提供商比特一致迁移；(6) incast感知的准入控制器在1.4倍负载下零缺失，过载时拒绝请求；(7) 无损GPU编解码器拓宽准入门限。

Result: 同节点缓存迁移18.8ms，比保存/加载快101倍；100G网络上维持92.1-94.8 Gb/s校验和验证吞吐；48次跨提供商迁移全部比特一致；incast感知准入控制器在1.4倍负载下零缺失。

Conclusion: 精确状态弹性是一个涉及传输和验证的联合调度问题。WorldMove展示了在控制配置下实现比特精确迁移的可行性，但服务循环和移动器的组合尚未在同一网络上构建。该工作提出了实时世界模型弹性调度的关键约束和解决方案。

Abstract: A persistent interactive world model keeps its running state resident on the GPU that serves it: a multi-gigabyte attention cache, almost all of it rewritten at every generation step. That state cannot be recomputed in interactive time or approximated without changing the world, so a live session pins its device. The pin is a scheduling problem. WorldMove moves a live session under one guarantee: the destination is bit-identical to the source, or nothing is installed. It relocates the cache in 18.8 ms same-node, 101x faster than save/load. It holds a checksum-verified 92.1-94.8 Gb/s on a 100 Gb fabric. At that rate the cache fits inside one interactive block. Migrating an actively generating session, it converges at a block boundary and the destination continues the world bit for bit. An admissibility condition decides each move. The move must complete inside the readout horizon, over bandwidth that covers the state plus its dirty rate. Lifted to a fleet schedulability test, it governed a consolidation loop that executed 48 of 48 migrations bit-identical across two providers. Two constraints are structural. Bit-exactness survives only inside a controlled configuration of one GPU architecture, so moving the state is the only way to preserve it exactly in interactive time. Verification cannot hide inside the wire on this fabric. Receive-path checksums stall the transport at protocol timescales under fan-in, and unscheduled incast silently collapses a receiver while every delivered byte stays correct. An incast-aware admission controller holds zero misses to 1.4x offered load and sheds overload as rejects. A lossless GPU codec widens the admission gate to fabrics raw motion cannot use. We exercise the serving loop and the mover separately, each end to end. Their composition on one fabric is unbuilt. Exact-state elasticity is a joint scheduling problem over transport and verification.

</details>


### [178] [CloudMicroHaskell: Direct-Style Distributed Haskell via Runtime Graph Serialisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10443)
*Robert Krook,Lennart Augustsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudMicroHaskell，一种基于MicroHaskell的Cloud Haskell风格库，通过运行时图序列化实现分布式编程的direct style，使远程进程可以直接捕获环境变量、消息可包含普通值（包括函数），无需手写闭包转换，并在微控制器异构部署中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloud Haskell将Erlang风格的分布式编程引入Haskell，但其对移动代码的处理在源码级API中暴露出困难边界：远程进程必须表达为静态闭包，消息必须满足序列化约束，参与节点需共享相关代码。作者希望探索不同的设计点，使远程spawn可以用direct style编写。

Method: 基于MicroHaskell构建，其运行时将代码和数据都表示为组合子图；当进程或消息跨越节点边界时，CloudMicroHaskell直接序列化可达图；实现了节点运行时（包括remote spawn、消息传递、监视器、退出传播）以及通用服务器和监督者等库。

Result: 通过进程/消息基准测试、分布式工作池基准测试、文件同步案例研究以及微控制器异构部署进行了评估，结果表明运行时图序列化使编程模型更直接。

Conclusion: CloudMicroHaskell的运行时图序列化使分布式编程更直接，但做出了明确的权衡：Cloud Haskell源码级类型保证的部分变为动态检查，程序员需注意惰性和运行时拥有的资源在节点间移动图时的处理。

Abstract: Cloud Haskell brings Erlang-style distributed programming to Haskell, but its treatment of mobile code exposes a difficult boundary in the source-level API. Remote processes must be expressed as static closures, messages must satisfy serialisation constraints, and participating nodes are assumed to share the relevant code.
  This paper explores a different design point. We present CloudMicroHaskell, a Cloud Haskell-style library built on MicroHaskell, whose runtime represents both code and data as a combinator graph. When a process or message crosses a node boundary, CloudMicroHaskell serialises the reachable graph directly. As a result, remote spawning can be written in direct style: process bodies may capture variables from their surrounding scope, and messages may contain ordinary values, including functions, without programmer-written closure conversion.
  We describe the implementation of the CloudMicroHaskell node runtime, including remote spawn, message delivery, monitors, exit propagation, and library implementations of generic servers and supervisors. We evaluate the system with process/message benchmarks, a distributed work-pool benchmark, a file-synchronisation case study, and a heterogeneous deployment on microcontrollers. The results show that runtime graph serialisation makes the \ch{} programming model substantially more direct, while also making the tradeoff explicit: some guarantees enforced by \ch{}'s source-level types become dynamic checks, and programmers must be aware of laziness and runtime-owned resources when moving graphs between nodes.

</details>


### [179] [Ichnos+: Estimating the Carbon Footprint of Scientific Workflows Using Fitted Power Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10586)
*Kathleen West,Youssef Moawad,Philipp Thamm,Vasilis Bountris,Giulio Attenni,Magnus Reid,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Ichnos+系统，用于量化Nextflow科学工作流的环境足迹（能耗、碳排放、隐含排放、水足迹和土地使用），其能耗估计误差为10.8%，显著优于现有方法，并支持扩展到其他工作流系统（如Apache Airflow）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流资源密集且长期运行，产生大量能耗和碳排放，但现有工具在共享/虚拟化环境中不可用，或仅基于少量通用数据点的功率模型，缺乏精确、可用的碳足迹量化方法。

Method: Ichnos+基于现有的工作流执行轨迹、特定于节点的计算资源功率模型以及与执行时间对齐的碳强度数据，进行事后足迹估算。支持估算运营碳、隐含排放、水足迹和土地使用。

Result: 在三个计算集群上，Ichnos+的能耗估计误差为10.8%，显著优于nf-core插件；并能估算隐含排放、水足迹和土地使用；还可扩展至Apache Airflow，保持高精度。

Conclusion: Ichnos+能够准确、全面地量化科学工作流的环境足迹，填补了现有工具在共享/虚拟化环境中的可用性缺口，并支持多维度环境影响评估和跨系统扩展。

Abstract: As data-intensive scientific workflows scale to facilitate the automation of analysis of increasing amounts of data, their resource-intensive and long-running execution incurs significant energy consumption and carbon emissions. Given the already significant and rising emissions from the ICT sector, it is crucial to quantify and understand the carbon footprint of scientific workflows. However, existing tooling is commonly not usable in shared, virtualized environments or resorts to power models that are based on only one or two generic data points.
  To address this gap, this paper presents Ichnos+, a novel system to quantify the environmental footprint of Nextflow scientific workflows. Ichnos+ enables post-hoc footprint estimation based on existing workflow traces, node-specific power models for the computational resources utilized, and carbon intensity data aligned with the execution time. We evaluate Ichnos+ against hardware-level energy measurements obtained using Intel RAPL, and the nf-core co2footprint plugin, which implements the Green Algorithms methodology. We find that Ichnos+ is capable of estimating workflow energy consumption with an estimation error of 10.8% across three compute clusters, significantly outperforming the nf-core plugin. We further show that Ichnos+ extends beyond operational carbon to estimate embodied emissions as well as water and land use. Finally, we demonstrate how Ichnos+ can be extended for another workflow system, Apache Airflow, maintaining a similarly high degree of estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [180] [[AAFLOW+] Stateful Operator Abstraction with Zero-Copy Distributed KV Cache Orchestration for Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10987)
*Arup Kumar Sarker,Alexander James Halpern,Mills Staylor,Aymen Alsaadi,Gregor von Laszewski,Yue Cheng,Shantenu Jha,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AAFLOW+是一个状态化的多智能体LLM系统扩展，将KV缓存作为一等分布式系统对象，通过零拷贝、传输感知的执行机制，使智能体间共享长上下文无需重复计算，显著降低TTFT（最高50.2倍）、多智能体计算成本（7.63倍）和KV内存（1.72-6.10倍），提升吞吐量（7.74倍以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体系统虽然集成了检索、规划和推理，但本质上仍是文本中心化的，智能体需要反复通过昂贵的预填充阶段重新计算共享上下文。虽然单请求推理可以通过KV缓存管理加速，但这通常局限于本地服务范围，缺乏分布式场景下的KV缓存共享机制。

Method: AAFLOW+将KV缓存提升为一等分布式系统对象，扩展了智能体工作流算子。具体包括：1）构建通信感知的计算图，同时优化数据、提示词和可复用模型状态；2）提供KV物化、传输、分叉、组合和驱逐等算子；3）运行时实现零拷贝、传输感知的执行机制，使智能体能复用长上下文而无需重新计算。

Result: 基于经验硬件微基准测试的分析成本模型评估显示：TTFT降低最多50.2倍，16智能体规模下多智能体计算成本降低7.63倍，KV内存减少1.72-6.10倍，吞吐量提升超过7.74倍。

Conclusion: 在中到高带宽网络上，KV传输优于重新计算，通过用KV状态共享替代文本传递，可以大幅提升多智能体LLM系统的效率。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly integrate retrieval, planning, and reasoning, but remain fundamentally text-centric, requiring agents to repeatedly recompute shared context through expensive prefill. Although single-request inference is known to be accelerated by KV-cache management, it is usually restricted to local serving scopes. We introduce AAFLOW+, a stateful extension of agentic workflow operators that makes KV cache a first-class distributed systems object. AAFLOW+ builds processes into communication-aware graphs that concurrently optimize data, prompts, and reusable model state. It also provides operators for KV materialization, transfer, fork, composition, and eviction. Its runtime enables zero-copy, transfer-aware execution, allowing agents to reuse long context without recomputation. AAFLOW+ reduces TTFT by up to 50.2x, achieves up to 7.63x reduced multi-agent compute cost at 16-agent scale, reduces KV memory by 1.72-6.10x, and increases throughput by more than 7.74x, based on an analytical cost model parameterized by empirical hardware microbenchmarks. The results demonstrate that KV transmission outperforms recomputation on networks with moderate to high bandwidth, making sure KV-state sharing greatly increases efficiency in multi-agent LLM systems by replacing text passing.

</details>


### [181] [Domain Extension of Lock-Freedom and Wait-Freedom for Group Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11014)
*Raaghav Ravishankar,Sandeep Kulkarni,Sathya Peri,Gokarna Sharma,Manaswini Piduguralla,Yashi Rastogi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了无锁和无等待计算概念的域扩展，将其适用范围从单线程任务扩展到多线程协作的群组计算（gl-freedom和gw-freedom），并通过理论一致性和实例验证了扩展定义的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的无锁和无等待计算定义仅适用于单线程完成任务（与其他线程竞争）的场景，无法描述多线程协作完成任务的计算模型。作者希望将这些概念扩展到群组计算领域，以更精确地刻画分布式计算、NUMA系统、私有数据多线程系统等新型计算场景中的并发特性，而不是简单地将它们归为死锁/活锁自由的同一类别。

Method: 作者采用定义域扩展的方法论，将无锁和无等待的定义从单线程域扩展到群组协作域，分别定义为gl-freedom和gw-freedom。扩展过程遵循三项约束：新域与原义保持逻辑一致、不引入矛盾或歧义、保留原定义的有效性和有用性。通过单线程群组的退化为原定义来验证一致性，并通过具体实例进行说明。

Result: 当群组仅包含单个线程时，扩展后的gl-freedom和gw-freedom退化为原有的无锁和无等待定义，证明了扩展的一致性；新定义能够刻画分布式计算、NUMA系统、私有数据多线程系统等场景中的并发性质，避免了将这些程序笼统地归为"死锁/活锁自由"而忽略其实际特性。

Conclusion: 域扩展是一种有效的定义泛化方法，可将锁自由和等待自由从单线程竞争域扩展到多线程协作域，为更广泛的计算模型提供了精确的并发性质刻画工具。

Abstract: A domain extension of a definition refers to broadening the scope of a definition so that it applies to a larger set of cases than originally specified. The notion of lock-free and wait-free computation is designed for the domain of tasks that are completed by a single thread (in competition with other threads). The goal of this paper is to extend the definition of lock-freedom and wait-freedom to group-computations (denoted by gl-freedom and gw-freedom) that require that the task at hand must be completed by a collaboration between multiple threads. When extending a definition, certain constraints must be respected: the new domain must remain logically consistent with the original meaning, the extension should not introduce contradictions or ambiguities, and it must preserve the essential properties that make the definition valid and useful. We demonstrate this by showing that our extended definition is consistent with the original definition when the group consists of a single thread. We note that extension allows us to characterize programs in a new domain (distributed computing, NUMA computation systems, systems with private data for different threads, etc.) instead of relegating them to be in the same category (deadlock/livelock-free) without regard to the actual properties of that program. We also illustrate this definition with various examples.

</details>


### [182] [Energy Calculus: A Compositional Algebra of Energy in Computational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11087)
*Mosharaf Chowdhury,Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Nishil Talati,Ruofan Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出能量演算（energy calculus），一种将能量视为一等公民的组合代数，通过能量元素、签名和三种组合算子（顺序、同设备并行、跨设备并行）对任意DAG结构计算进行能量建模，并基于七条公理和归约定理实现带误差边界的能量预测与Pareto前沿推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI扩展受能量约束，但能量缺乏像计算、通信、学习那样的形式化处理。现有节能方案针对特定粒度和结构，无法组合推理其联合效应，能量仍是事后测量的标量，缺乏通用优化方法。

Method: 构建能量演算的组合代数：定义能量元素（带能量签名：时间、静态/动态能量、硬件工作点、执行上下文、测量不确定性）；定义三种算子（顺序、同设备并行、跨设备并行）；建立七条公理描述硬件能耗行为；提出归约定理，当交互低于测量不确定性时恢复简单的上下文无关代数；不确定性通过组合传播，每个预测带误差边界；算子从能量总量扩展到时间-能量Pareto前沿的组合推理。

Result: 该代数揭示了能量的两个独特性质：顺序组合仅在元素互不上下文敏感时交换，且顺序组合不分配并行组合。归约定理允许在物理允许时回归简单代数。不确定性传播机制保证了预测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 能量演算为AI系统提供了一种形式化、可组合的能量建模框架，使能量从事后测量的标量变为可组合推理的一等公民，支持跨粒度、跨结构的能量优化分析，且能扩展到时间-能量的多目标权衡推理。

Abstract: Energy is a binding constraint for AI scaling, yet it lacks the formal treatment that computation, communication, and learning have long enjoyed. Recent systems demonstrate large energy savings, but each targets a specific granularity and structure; one cannot combine frequency scaling from one system with critical-path analysis from another and reason about their joint effect on total energy. Energy remains a monolithic scalar that is measured after the fact and optimized with point solutions that do not generalize.
  We propose energy calculus, a compositional algebra that treats energy as a first-class primitive. It builds on energy elements, units of computation whose energy we can reliably measure, each carrying an energy signature that comprises its time, its static and dynamic energy, the hardware operating point and execution context under which we measured it, and the associated measurement uncertainty. Three operators (sequential, same-device parallel, and cross-device parallel) compose signatures along the same structure as the computation itself, covering arbitrary DAG-structured executions. The algebra rests on seven axioms that capture how hardware consumes energy, and it exhibits two properties distinctive to energy among computing resources: sequential composition commutes only when elements are mutually context-insensitive, and sequential composition does not distribute over parallel composition. We also present a Reduction Theorem that recovers simple context-independent algebra whenever interactions fall below measurement uncertainty, so practitioners pay for context dependence only where the physics demands it. Uncertainty propagates through every composition, so each prediction carries an error bound. Finally, we show that the same operators extend from energy totals to time--energy Pareto frontiers, so reasoning about tradeoffs composes with the same algebra.

</details>


### [183] [Xema: Efficient Diffusion Serving through Fine-Grained Memory Management and Auto-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11136)
*Xueze Kang,Guangyu Xiang,Suyi Li,Yuxin Wang,Shaohuai Shi,Lin Zhang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xema是一个面向扩散模型的高效服务系统，利用可预测的张量生命周期进行离线追踪引导的内存优化，通过选择性内存缓解和静态内存布局，在满足GPU内存和SLO约束的同时联合优化并行度、并发度和内存控制，显著提升SLO达成率并降低规划成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在部署高分辨率图像和长视频生成服务时，GPU内存成为瓶颈。现有内存节省技术（如权重卸载、分片、VAE切片）常引入显著性能开销，不适用于生产环境。

Method: Xema为每个请求模板生成离线内存追踪，识别短时内存压力区间并仅在这些区间内按需应用内存缓解；构建静态内存布局以减少碎片化预留内存；设计离线规划器，在GPU内存和SLO约束下联合选择并行度、并发度和内存控制策略，将规划结果存入计划表供在线运行时直接使用。

Result: 在Flux.2、CogVideoX-5B和LTX-2上评估，相比现有服务配置，SLO达成率提升最高3.7倍，规划成本从6.3小时降至197秒（相比网格搜索）。

Conclusion: 利用扩散模型中张量生命周期的可预测性进行离线追踪引导的内存优化，能够在不引入显著运行时开销的前提下有效缓解GPU内存压力，提升服务性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly deployed as production visual-generation services, where serving high-resolution image and long video generation is often limited by GPU memory. Popular memory-saving techniques such as weight offloading, sharding, and VAE slicing are often not practical because they tend to introduce significant performance overhead. In this paper, we present Xema, a memory-efficient diffusion serving system that exploits predictable tensor lifetimes for trace-guided memory optimization. For each request template, Xema derives an offline memory trace to identify short memory-pressure intervals and applies memory mitigation only within these intervals and only by the amount needed to fit the target GPU budget. Xema further constructs a static memory layout for tensors with predictable lifetimes, reducing fragmentation-induced reserved memory and making offline memory reasoning reliable at runtime. Built on this memory optimization layer, Xema introduces an offline planner that jointly selects parallelism, concurrency, and memory control under GPU memory and SLO constraints. The selected plan is stored in a plan table and directly used by the online serving runtime. We implement Xema on production diffusion pipelines and evaluate it with Flux.2, CogVideoX-5B, and LTX-2. Compared with existing serving configurations, Xema improves SLO attainment by up to 3.7x and reduces planning cost from 6.3 hours to 197 seconds compared with grid search.

</details>


### [184] [GPU-Tile-Sim: A Tile-Centric GPU Simulation Framework for LLM Hardware-Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11262)
*Yitong Ding,Jiawei Huang,Renyang Guan,Yangjie Zhou,Zihan Liu,Yu Feng,Shixuan Sun,Mingyi Guo,Jingwen Leng,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种以 tile 为中心的 GPU 模拟框架 GPU-Tile-Sim，通过瓦片图表示 LLM 内核的依赖结构与执行调度，实现高精度性能建模，MAPE 为 1.22%-8.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GPU 性能模型面临两难：指令级模拟器适应新架构成本高，而分析模型过于粗糙，无法捕捉 LLM 内核细粒度依赖调度与计算-内存重叠等特性。

Method: 将内核执行表示为 warp 级瓦片图，节点为瓦片级操作，边编码数据与顺序约束；设计自动瓦片图前端和图驱动模拟后端。

Result: 在 A100 和 H100 上的 GEMM、注意力机制及端到端 LLM 推理任务中，对常规和高度优化内核均达到 1.22%-8.71% 的 MAPE，并初步验证了对 Blackwell 的扩展能力。

Conclusion: GTSim 可有效分析软件与架构设计选择，为 LLM 硬件-软件协同设计提供高效精确的性能建模工具。

Abstract: Modern LLM (large language model) workloads increasingly rely on optimized GPU kernels through hardware-software co-design. These kernels achieve high-performance through fine-grained dependency scheduling and computation-memory overlap. As such, they incur new challenges on existing GPU performance models. Instruction-driven simulators are costly to adapt to evolving architectures, while analytical models are too coarse to capture kernels' characteristics. We propose GPU-Tile-Sim, a tile-centric GPU simulation framework for LLM hardware-software co-design. The key insight is that modern LLM kernel performance is governed less by individual instruction latency than by the dependency structure that controls execution order and overlap. Accordingly, GTSim represents kernel execution as a warp-level tile graph whose nodes capture tile-level operations and whose edges encode data and ordering constraints. Using this representation, we design an automatic tile-graph frontend and a graph-driven simulation backend. We evaluate GTSim on representative GEMM, attention, and end-to-end LLM inference workloads. On A100 and H100 across both conventional and highly optimized kernels, GTSim achieves high performance-modeling accuracy (MAPE, Mean Absolute Percentage Error, 1.22%--8.71%). We further extend GTSim to Blackwell with preliminary validation, and demonstrate its effectiveness in analyzing software and architectural design choices.

</details>


### [185] [Decomposing Runtime, Kernel, and Quantization Speedups via a Matched FP16 Intermediate: A Hardware-Conditioned Case Study on Four NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11368)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过消融研究，将量化推理加速分解为运行时改进与量化内核贡献两部分。实验表明，运行时改进贡献了约三分之二的加速（对数尺度），而量化内核贡献相对稳定。同时分析了多卡分片效率、NVLink vs PCIe带宽影响、量化对并发用户数的提升以及采样模式差异对结果有效性的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化加速通常被报告为一个整体数字（权重格式+内核+运行时），缺乏各组成部分贡献的分解。本文旨在量化运行时与量化内核各自的贡献，并分析多卡分片、链路带宽等因素对推理性能的影响。

Method: 在4张NVIDIA RTX A5000（24GB，NVLink桥接）上进行消融研究：构建匹配的中间栈（保留快速运行时但去除量化内核），将完整加速分解为运行时部分和内核+量化部分。使用贪婪解码、profiler追踪、NVLink vs PCIe对比控制等实验设计。

Result: 完整栈端到端加速2.58倍，运行时贡献约三分之二（对数尺度）；内核+量化部分在三类相似模型家族中变化不超过1.5%；单实例跨4卡分片性能未翻倍，约80%的每token短尾归因于协调开销；NVLink与PCIe实测带宽相近；量化使可持续并发用户数约为半精度内存悬崖的4倍。

Conclusion: 运行时优化对推理加速贡献更大且稳定，量化内核贡献相对较小；多卡分片受限于协调开销而非链路带宽；小模型的分片与多实例选择取决于工作负载，大模型更偏好双卡配对实例；量化可显著提升并发用户数。

Abstract: Reported serving speedups from quantized kernels typically bundle the weight format, the kernel, and the inference runtime into one number. We present an attribution study on four NVIDIA RTX A5000 GPUs, 24 GiB each, on a single host with NVLink-bridged pairs. A matched intermediate stack that keeps the faster runtime without the quantized kernel splits the full speedup into a runtime part and a kernel and quantization part. Under matched greedy decoding the full stack reaches $2.58\times$ end to end, with the runtime change accounting for about two thirds of that gain on a logarithmic scale; across three similar model families the kernel and quantization part moves by at most 1.5%. Sharding one instance across all four cards falls well below doubling: a profiler trace attributes about 80% of the per token shortfall to coordination, and an NVLink versus PCIe control on the same hardware shows similar realized bandwidth on both links, pointing away from link bandwidth as the cause. Whether to run one sharded instance or several independent ones depends on the workload and the model, with the ranking reversing on the larger model: the smaller model splits between sharding and multiple instances by workload, while the larger model favors two paired instances on every workload. Quantization extends sustainable concurrent users roughly four times past a reproducible half precision memory cliff. Differences in sampling mode and prompt pool between the two stacks are documented as threats to validity.

</details>


### [186] [Stage-Level Executor Allocation in Apache Spark with Cost-Performance Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11415)
*Miriam Rateike,Isaac Waweru Wambugu,Celia Cintas,Michael Kaufmann,Ioana Giurgiu,Skyler Speakman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种在无服务器Apache Spark环境中按阶段动态分配执行器数量的方法，通过树集成模型预测运行时间和成本，支持用户自定义成本-性能权衡，在TPC-DS和SQLStorm基准测试中实现了显著的成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式处理系统的执行器分配方法要么在应用级别进行粗粒度分配（导致子优），要么无法支持用户对成本-性能权衡的显式偏好（如时间预算内最小化成本）。

Method: 使用树集成模型（估计各阶段运行时间和成本作为资源分配的函数），基于这些估计为每个阶段单独推荐执行器数量，允许用户指定成本-性能权衡参数。

Result: 在TPC-DS（103个查询）上实现约50%成本节省，仅约16%速度下降；在SQLStorm（96个查询）上实现约40.5%成本节省，约29%速度下降。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效实现用户定义的成本-性能权衡，在各阶段粒度动态分配资源，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Allocating executors (i.e. compute resources) to distributed processing systems must balance resource costs of scaling-out unnecessarily against artificial, performance-limiting bottlenecks. Naive approaches may allocate executors at the application level, which have predictable costs and performance but are almost guaranteed to be sub-optimal for each of the thousands of diverse, individual stages executed by the application. Users may also have explicit preferences, such as completing an application within a specific time budget while minimizing cost, that existing solutions usually fail to support. We propose a novel method for determining the number of executors per stage in a serverless Apache Spark environment, enabling users to specify their desired cost-performance tradeoff. Our approach trains tree-ensemble models to estimate the run times and costs of a stage as a function of allocated resources. These estimates are then used to recommend resources for each stage individually. We evaluate our approach on TPC-DS and SQLStorm benchmarks and compare it against two baselines. Depending on the user-defined trade-off parameter and setup, our approach achieves approx. 50% cost savings across 103 TPC-DS queries with only a approx. 16% slowdown, and approx. 40.5% on 96 SQLStorm queries at a approx. 29% slowdown.

</details>


### [187] [MemExchange: Cloud-Scale Memory Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11579)
*AmirHossein Seyri,Abhisek Pan,Balajee Vamanan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MemExchange是一个基于RDMA的集群级多租户内存管理系统，通过在线缺失率曲线估计和边际效用分配，动态调整内存缓存租户的分配量，将分散的空闲内存聚合成逻辑共享池，实现跨节点内存交换，提升集群内存利用率并降低受限租户的缓存缺失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云提供商过度预配置内存以满足峰值需求，导致数据中心集群内存利用率低下；同时内存受限的租户即使在其他节点有空闲内存时仍遭受高缓存缺失率，存在内存资源"孤岛"问题。

Method: 基于在线缺失率曲线(MRC)估计的边际效用分配算法，结合RDMA实现跨节点内存交换；设计MemExchange Tracker Communication (MTC)协议支持应用层协调和一端RDMA操作，无需远程CPU参与。

Result: 相比TCP方案降低2.3倍远程访问开销，机架规模集群内存利用率提升13%，内存受限租户在偏斜工作负载下缺失率降低高达63%。

Conclusion: MemExchange通过分布式内存聚合与智能动态分配，在不强制租户共置或无中心化协调的条件下，有效缓解了内存资源碎片化问题，显著提升集群整体性能和资源效率。

Abstract: To handle unpredictable workloads, cloud providers typically over-provision memory to meet peak demand, resulting in substantial underutilization across datacenter clusters. At the same time, memory-constrained tenants may suffer elevated cache miss rates, even when idle capacity remains stranded elsewhere in the infrastructure. MemExchange is a cluster-wide, multi-tenant memory management system that dynamically right-sizes in-memory caching tenants according to workload demand. Leveraging marginal-utility-based allocation derived from online Miss Ratio Curve (MRC) estimation, MemExchange redistributes idle memory between tenants across physical nodes using RDMA. This approach transforms the dedicated caching memory scattered across servers into a logically aggregated pool, enabling cross-node memory exchange without centralized coordination or forced tenant co-location. To support efficient remote access, we design the MemExchange Tracker Communication (MTC) protocol, an application-layer mechanism that coordinates memory reallocation and enables one-sided RDMA operations without involving remote CPUs. We implement MemExchange in Memcached and evaluate it through microbenchmarks, medium and rack-scale deployments of up to 100 CloudLab servers. Our results show up to 2.3x lower remote-access overhead compared to TCP-based designs, a 13% increase in cluster-wide memory utilization at rack scale, and up to 63% reduction in miss rate for memory-constrained tenants under skewed workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [From ML Predictions to Informed Diagnostic Assistance Using the Toulmin Model of Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09664)
*Anca Marginean,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Toulmin论证模型将视网膜图像诊断分解为主张、依据、保证、限定词、反驳和支撑六个组件，通过专用生物标志提取模型、MedGemma医学知识代理、MedSigLip图像相似度等模块构建可解释的诊断框架，使人类专家能更批判性地评估ML生成的诊断结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML模型生成的诊断结果缺乏可解释性和结构化评估，单纯依赖XAI方法仍难以提供足够的可信度，需要一种更系统、可解释的论证框架来辅助人类专家判断。

Method: 将Toulmin论证模型应用于图像诊断，使用生物标志提取模型作为依据（grounds），MedGemma代理作为保证（warrant）连接依据与主张，基于定量评估确定限定词（qualifier），利用MedSigLip图像相似度构建反驳（rebuttal），最终将所有组件呈现给人类专家。

Result: 构建了包含claim、grounds、warrant、qualifier、rebuttal和backing六个组件的完整论证框架，每个组件均有对应的ML模块实现，形成了结构化的可解释诊断流程。

Conclusion: 该论证框架使人类专家能够对ML生成的诊断进行更知情和更批判性的评估，提升了诊断过程的可解释性和可信度。

Abstract: To provide a structured and interpretable assessment, we decompose the image-based diagnosis into components following the Toulmin model of argumentation. This model consists of a claim, grounds, warrant, qualifier, rebuttal, and backing. Consider a claim generated by a machine learning (ML) model for retinal diagnosis. Rather than accepting this claim at face value, one could either apply explainable AI (XAI) methods or adopt an argumentation-based approach. In our framework, a model specialized in biomarker extraction from images provides the grounds. The warrant-linking the grounds to the claim - is analyzed by an agent equipped with medical knowledge; in our architecture, this role is fulfilled by a MedGemma agent. The qualifier is determined based on the overall quantitative evaluation of both the warrant and grounds models. Finally, a rebuttal is constructed using image similarity measures computed with MedSigLip. All these components are presented to the human expert, enabling a more informed and critical assessment of the ML-generated diagnosis.

</details>


### [189] [Format Sensitivity Index: Token-Controlled Prompt Wrapper Robustness and Schema Compliance in LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09665)
*Deep Pankajbhai Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提示词包装器仅格式不同即可改变模型分数，甚至颠覆排行榜结论。本文提出格式敏感性指数（FSI）和可解析性敏感性指数（PSI），通过对140,000次生成实验的分析，发现FSI在模型间变化超过30倍，且主要由合规失败解释。固定效应回归表明，即使控制任务、模型和包装器后，可解析性仍是准确率的强预测因子。建议报告准确率时应同时报告包装器方差和合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示提示词包装器（Prompt wrappers）仅因格式差异就能显著影响模型性能，导致排行榜结论不可靠，需要量化这种敏感性并给出改进建议。

Method: 提出FSI（格式敏感性指数，衡量准确率范围）和PSI（可解析性敏感性指数，衡量答案可解析性范围）两个互补指标。在token控制协议下，使用140,000次OpenRouter生成实验，涵盖7个QA任务、5个包装器家族、4个指令微调模型（7B-72B参数），并进行固定效应回归分析。

Result: 不同模型间平均FSI差异超过30倍，主要由合规失败（compliance failures）解释。固定效应回归显示，即使控制任务、模型和包装器因素后，可解析性仍是准确率的强预测因子。

Conclusion: 仅报告准确率而不考虑包装器方差和合规性在统计上是脆弱的，研究给出针对基准测试和结构化输出部署的实用建议。

Abstract: Prompt wrappers often differ only in formatting, yet they can change model scores enough to flip leaderboard conclusions. We study this variance under a token-controlled protocol and introduce two complementary metrics: the Format Sensitivity Index (FSI), the accuracy range induced by wrapper choice, and the Parseability Sensitivity Index (PSI), the corresponding range in answer parseability. Across 140,000 OpenRouter generations spanning 7 QA tasks, 5 wrapper families, and 4 instruct models from 7B to 72B parameters, we find that mean FSI varies by over 30x across models and is largely explained by compliance failures. A fixed-effects regression shows that parseability remains a strong predictor of accuracy even after controlling for task, model, and wrapper. We argue that reporting accuracy without wrapper variance and compliance is statistically fragile, and we give practical recommendations for both benchmarking and structured-output deployments.

</details>


### [190] [Faithful, Not Corrective: Message-Format Effects in Multi-Hop Agent Relays Are Tier-Dependent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09678)
*Zayx Shawn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过多跳接力实验发现，消息格式对LLM agent间信息传递的影响取决于中继（relay）能力。强中继下格式差异极小，弱中继下格式排名会翻转，结构化格式（如JSON）能提高保真度但需付出编码代价，且错误一旦注入便难以消除——格式只是忠实、错误局域化的通道，而非纠错码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作对LLM agent之间消息格式的影响存在矛盾观点：格式优化工作认为结构化消息能降低成本且不损失准确性，而格式限制工作认为施加结构会降低生成质量。然而，这些研究都忽略了多跳场景中复制保真度（copy fidelity）才是关键问题，而非单次生成。因此需要系统研究不同格式在多跳接力中的影响。

Method: 设计了受控接力测试平台：将12个程序化生成的原子事实在5种格式（自由NL、精确指令NL、JSON、三元组、键值对）下进行6跳的逐跳重新编码，由固定的强评分器根据程序化真实值打分，涵盖两种中继能力层级、认知负荷条件和配对分叉错误注入。

Result: (i) 强中继下信息几乎无损，无"电话游戏"式崩溃，认知负荷加入后保真度变化在±1.8个百分点内，但生成成本上升24-53%；(ii) 弱中继（1.5B）下6跳召回率的格式间差距从2.3扩大到20.5个百分点（扩大8.7倍），原因是刚性格式的编码代价与固定键JSON模式的抗漂移性两个对立机制导致格式排名在传递过程中翻转；(iii) 配对分叉注入后，错误值在83-100%的链中持续到最终跳，与真实值的保留率高度一致，且未对邻近事实造成附带损害。

Conclusion: 结构化格式提供的是忠实、错误局域化的通道，而非纠错码；格式选择应取决于管道中最弱的中继环节。

Abstract: When LLM agents hand off information to one another, does the message format matter? Two literatures disagree: format-optimization work reports that structured messages cut cost without hurting accuracy, while format-restriction work finds that imposing structure degrades generation -- and neither measures what happens when a message traverses multiple hops, where copy fidelity, not one-shot generation, dominates. We introduce a controlled relay testbed: briefs of twelve programmatically generated atomic facts are re-encoded hop-by-hop in five formats (free NL, precision-instructed NL, JSON, triples, key-value) over six hops, scored by a fixed strong grader against programmatic ground truth, across two relay-capability tiers, a cognitive-load condition, and a paired-fork error injection. We find that message-format effects are tier-dependent. (i) Under faithful-relay instructions a strong relay is nearly lossless -- the documented "telephone-game" collapse does not occur -- and adding per-hop cognitive load leaves format-level fidelity unchanged (within +/-1.8 points) while raising generation cost by 24-53%. (ii) Under a weak (1.5B) relay the across-format spread of six-hop recall grows by a factor of 8.7 (from 2.3 to 20.5 points), driven by two opposing mechanisms -- an encoding toll paid by the rigid formats and drift resistance specific to the fixed-key JSON schema -- that flip the format ranking in transit. (iii) In a paired-fork injection, an injected wrong value, once present, persists to the final hop in 83-100% of chains in every format, closely matching each format's retention of the true value, with no detectable collateral damage to neighboring facts. Structure buys a faithful, error-localizing channel -- not an error-correcting code -- and format choice should follow the weakest relay in the pipeline.

</details>


### [191] [Boltzmann MapReduce: A Partition-Function Reduce for Forkable Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09689)
*Yossi Eliaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在局部渐近正态（LAN）框架下，工作节点输出的置信密度为Gibbs-Boltzmann分布，温度T=1/n。MapReduce的reduce操作对应配分函数Z，其众数为精度加权池化，频率学派一致性对应零温极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将统计推断中的置信密度与统计力学中的Boltzmann分布建立联系，揭示MapReduce框架的统计基础，并连接贝叶斯推断、频率学派一致性与物理中的热力学极限。

Method: 基于局部渐近正态（LAN）理论，将n个样本的置信密度表达为Gibbs-Boltzmann形式exp{-nE(θ)}，其中逆温度β=n。在Gauss/线性情形下精确成立，一般情形一阶近似。

Result: 1) 不重叠数据块的Boltzmann因子独立；2) MapReduce的reduce操作对应配分函数Z=∫∏_k h_k dθ，其众数为精度加权（逆方差）池化；3) 频率学派一致性对应零温极限T=1/n→0。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了分布式统计推断、贝叶斯分析和统计力学，为理解MapReduce等分布式计算范式提供了严格的统计理论基础。

Abstract: To leading order under local asymptotic normality (LAN), the confidence density a worker emits over a chunk of size $n$ is a Gibbs--Boltzmann measure $\exp\{-βE(θ)\}$ whose inverse temperature is the sample size, $β=n$. Three consequences are exact in the Gaussian/linear case and first-order otherwise: disjoint chunks carry independent Boltzmann factors, so the MapReduce \emph{reduce}, read literally, is a partition function $Z=\int\prod_k h_k\,dθ$ whose mode is precision-weighted (inverse-variance) pooling; frequentist consistency is the zero-temperature limit $T=1/n\to0$

</details>


### [192] [Interpreting Latent CoT Reasoning as Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09698)
*Sabari Iyyappan Duraipandian,Shreya Sanjay Boyane,Manju Nagesh,Jerome Francis,Archana Vaidheeswaran,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过动力系统分析（步长变化、方向一致性、Lyapunov指数等）揭示了潜在思维链（latent CoT）中CODI和COCONUT两种方法的推理动态：CODI呈现稳定吸引子行为，COCONUT呈现不稳定扩张行为，SIM-CoT监督会强化这两种行为而不改变其底层动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法（如CODI、COCONUT）在隐空间中同时维护多条叠加候选轨迹，缺乏可解释性，且现有机制分析方法无法解释推理在潜在步骤间如何演化。

Method: 将潜在token序列建模为表示空间中的轨迹，应用动力系统分析（步间变化、方向一致性、Lyapunov敏感性），结合定性投影（UMAP、DMD/PHATE）来刻画推理演化。

Result: 潜在CoT展现出结构化的非随机动态，存在两类不同的稳定性：CODI为稳定吸引子，COCONUT为不稳定扩张系统；SIM-CoT监督强化两类行为但不改变底层动态。

Conclusion: 该框架推进了潜在CoT推理动态的可解释性，并为改进潜在推理性能提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods, such as CODI and COCONUT, face a fundamental interpretability problem: they maintain multiple superimposed candidate traces in the hidden space at each step, unlike explicit- CoT, which follows a single transparent reasoning trace. Existing mechanistic methods show compression, shortcuts, and superposition without explaining how reasoning evolves across latent steps. To address this gap, we model latent token sequences as trajectories in representation space and apply dynamical systems analysis to characterize the evolution of reasoning. Using quantitative measures, such as step-to-step change, direction consistency, and Lyapunov sensitivity, alongside qualitative projections, such as UMAP and DMD/PHATE, we show that latent CoT exhibits structured, non-random dynamics with two distinct stability classes. CODI behaves as a stable attractor, while COCONUT behaves as an unstable expanding system, and SIM-CoT supervision tightens both behaviors without changing the underlying dynamics. This framework advances the interpretability of latent CoT reasoning dynamics and provides actionable insights for improving latent reasoning performance. Code1 and Project page2 available online.

</details>


### [193] [YUKTI: From Natural-Language Situations to Robust, Verifiable Decisions An Uncertainty-Typed Proposition IR, Assumption-Robust Pareto Frontiers, and a Regret Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09706)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YUKTI提出了一种新的自动公式化表示方法，通过类型化命题图、系数不确定性和分布帕累托交接，生成对假设鲁棒的决策计划，在多种验证场景下显著降低遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型驱动的决策管线（NL4Opt、OptiMUS、ORLM等）将自然语言情景转化为单一目标、点值系数的数值规划，然后单次求解。这种"一次求解"范式在分配真实预算、努力或临床注意力时是脆弱的——每个客观化的数字都是一个假设，只有在猜测完全正确时才最优，这本质上是计算的模仿而非真正的决策智能。

Method: YUKTI改变了自动公式化的目标：(1) 将输入表示为类型化命题图，其关系携带形状先验、系数不确定性和来源追溯；(2) 将每个阶段路由到精确、非线性或进化求解器；(3) 通过分布帕累托交接耦合各阶段；(4) 引入假设鲁棒帕累托前沿（ARPF），通过重采样假设（包括结构ε-污染）评估每个行动存活的频率（rho）。同时证明rho是决策遗憾的精确因子，并构建了基准数据集SRJANA。

Result: (1) 在受控错误设定下，鲁棒折衷方案相比朴素点计划将均值和尾部遗憾降低90%以上；(2) 在受监管的商业决策中，在合法行动空间内优化并以欧元定价下行风险；(3) 在41,188个真实决策数据集上，样本外回测比记录的状态提升34%，比朴素点规则提升4%，同时减少了优化者的诅咒。LLM即使给出正确数字、单目标优化，其保留遗憾约为YUKTI的47倍。

Conclusion: YUKTI证明了决策的鲁棒性不应来自更好的求解器，而应来自更好的公式化表示——承认并量化不确定性。LLM的角色是公式化者（formulator）而非求解者（solver）。论文还指出了前向交接在长程因果耦合下的局限性，需要转向后向归纳因果策略。

Abstract: Language models turn a worded situation into a numeric plan, and the dominant pipelines (NL4Opt, OptiMUS, ORLM, OR-LLM-Agent) commit to a single objective and point-valued coefficients, then solve once. For decisions that allocate real budget, effort, or clinical attention, that confidence is the failure mode: every objectified number is an assumption, and a plan optimal only if the guesses are exactly right is fragile -- mimicry of computation. YUKTI changes the target of autoformulation. Its representation is a typed-proposition graph whose relationships carry shape priors, coefficient uncertainty, and provenance. YUKTI routes each stage to an exact, nonlinear, or evolutionary solver; couples stages by a distributional Pareto hand-off; and introduces Assumption-Robust Pareto Frontiers (ARPF), resampling assumptions (including structural epsilon-contamination) to score how often each action survives (rho). We prove a bound making rho an exact factor of decision regret, add auditable traceability, and synthesize a benchmark-faithful data foundation when none exists (SRJANA). We validate three ways: under controlled misspecification the robust compromise cuts mean and tail regret by over 90% versus a naive point plan; on a regulated commercial decision we optimize inside a lawful action space and price the downside in euros; and on a real public dataset of 41,188 decisions an out-of-sample backtest beats the logged status quo by 34% and a naive point rule by 4% while reducing the optimizer's curse. The solvers are standard; we claim no benchmark-SOTA win. A head-to-head shows an LLM given the correct numbers, and single-objective optimization, both incur about 47x the held-out regret of YUKTI -- an LLM is a formulator, not a solver. Under long-range causal coupling, the forward hand-off becomes unsound, locating where it must become a backward-induction causal policy.

</details>


### [194] [GES-TSP: Graph Edge Sparsification for TSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09708)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的图边稀疏化方法GES，用于欧几里得TSP问题。通过结合几何结构信息和组合优化技术，GES能自适应生成稀疏化图，在MATILDA数据集上可剪枝高达95%的边且最优间隙保持在1%以内，在TSPLIB上泛化能力强，大规模实例剪枝率超99%而最优间隙仍低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确求解大规模TSP实例计算开销巨大，传统图稀疏化方法依赖固定启发式规则，未能充分利用实例特定的结构信息，导致稀疏化效率受限。

Method: 提出GES（Graph Edge Sparsification），一种基于学习的边稀疏化方法。该方法融合几何结构信息（如欧几里得距离等）与组合优化技术，针对不同实例自适应生成稀疏化图，从而显著缩减图规模并加速求解过程。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上可剪枝高达95%的边，最优间隙保持在1%以内；在TSPLIB基准上展现出强泛化能力，部分大规模实例剪枝率超过99%，最优间隙仍低于1%。

Conclusion: GES方法通过自适应学习实例结构信息，在显著降低TSP图规模的同时保持了接近最优的解质量，为大规模TSP的高效精确求解提供了有效途径，且具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99%, while the optimality gap remains below 1%.

</details>


### [195] [The Verifier is the Curriculum: Execution-Gated Self-Distillation for Cross-Family Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09709)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用不可欺骗的确定性过滤器（严格启动）对代码生成器进行拒绝采样自蒸馏，能显著提升模型在未见过游戏家族上的生成能力，而可欺骗的代理指标或简单增加数据量均无法带来同等效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码生成后训练方法常使用可学习的判分器进行优化，但这会导致模型学习代理特征（提高分数而不改进实际质量）。作者希望研究一种相反的信号：确定性、无判分器、不可欺骗的过滤器，以验证其是否能带来真正的泛化提升。
